3. Data Gaps: Public vs. Restricted vs. Commercial-Vendor Sources

A central finding of Task 01 is the structural boundary between publicly accessible government/developer data and authentication-gated or commercially licensed datasets across the seven Emirates.

3.1 What Is Publicly Accessible (Open Data & Public Portals)

  • Dubai (Dubai Pulse & DLD Open Data):
  • Open & Downloadable (CSV/JSON/API): Buildings registry, Units registry, Projects (Mashrooi: project number, master developer, escrow agent bank, % completion, status, start/completion dates), Sales Transactions, Rent Contracts (Ejari), Valuation, and Accredited Developers & Brokers.
  • GIS Layers (GIS.gov.ae & Makani): Parcel boundaries, 10-digit Makani building entrance coordinates, and DDA Master Plan zoning overlays.
  • Abu Dhabi (DMT Open Data, DARI, ADREC, SCAD):
  • Open & Viewable: Plot-by-plot cadastral fabric, sector/zone boundaries, base floorplate limits, land-use zoning on SmartHub/Onwani, registered off-plan project & escrow index on DARI, and quarterly SCAD construction material price indices.
  • Qudurat (DARI): Public searchable directory of classified contractors (Special, First to Sixth Grade) and engineering consultants.
  • Sharjah, Ajman, Ras Al Khaimah, Fujairah & Umm Al Quwain:
  • Sharjah (SRERD Aqari & SDTPS): Public searchable directory of licensed developers, escrow accounts, and parcel validation map (maps.sdtps.gov.ae).
  • Ajman (AJRE & gis.ajman.ae): Quarterly real estate reports, licensed developer/project search, and public parcel explorer.
  • Ras Al Khaimah (RAK Municipality & RAK GIS): Public contractor/consultant classification registry, Barjeel green building guidelines, and Marjan Island investment zone briefs.

3.2 What Is Restricted Behind UAE PASS, Escrow Login, or Commercial Licenses (Data Gaps)

  • Plot-Level Building Permits & Approved Engineering Drawings (IFC / BIM / CAD):
  • Across all 7 Emirates (Build in Dubai, DMT MePS, Sharjah Engineering Portal, Unified Northern Emirates MePS), actual building permit applications, structural/MEP drawings, soil investigation reports, and Completion Certificates are strictly restricted to the Plot Owner (via UAE PASS) and the Appointed Consultant/Contractor of Record. They cannot be legally scraped or accessed without owner authorization.
  • Itemized Contract Bills of Quantities (BOQs) & Tender Commercial Submissions:
  • Private real estate developers (Emaar, Aldar, DAMAC, Nakheel, Sobha, Arada) do not publish itemized contract BOQs or winning contractor unit rates (AED/m³ concrete, AED/tonne rebar installed) publicly.
  • While government tender notices appear on Abu Dhabi DGP (Al Maqtaa), Dubai eSupply, and UAE Federal DPP, the downloadable tender BOQ packages (8–25 CSI divisions) require approved vendor pre-qualification and tender fee payment.
  • Utility Load Allocations & NOC Conditions (DEWA, TAQA, SEWA, EtihadWE):
  • Specific substation load approvals (MVA), chilled-water connection capacities (TR), and plot-level infrastructure NOC conditions are restricted to licensed MEP/civil consultants via utility portals.
  • Northern Emirates Open Data Granularity Gap (Fujairah & Umm Al Quwain):
  • Unlike Dubai (Dubai Pulse) and Abu Dhabi (DMT/DARI), neither Fujairah Municipality nor Umm Al Quwain Municipality currently publishes bulk machine-readable CSV/JSON open data feeds of active construction permits or unit-by-unit escrow progress. Coverage in these two emirates relies on master developer disclosures (Sobha Realty, Eagle Hills, Deyaar, Taaleem) and federal infrastructure tenders (MoEI, Etihad Rail).

4. Critical Verification Finding: Audit of the ~AED 250 / sq.ft. Cost Lead

Section 9 of the Task 01 specification states:

"Market leads indicate that average construction cost in the UAE may be around ~250 AED per sq. ft., though actual costs vary depending on building type, materials, location and project scale. Verify whether this figure holds across segments..."

4.1 Empirical Verification Conclusion

Cross-referencing Turner & Townsend (ICMS 2024–2025 & GCMI 2025–2026), Stonehaven RICS UAE Market Report (2025), Currie & Brown, Rider Levett Bucknall (RLB), and Knight Frank (2025) proves that AED 250 / sq.ft. (AED 2,691 / sq.m. or USD 733 / sq.m.) does NOT hold as a turnkey construction cost average for UAE residential or commercial buildings in 2025–2026:

  • Verified Turnkey All-Type Average: AED 657–679 / sq.ft. (USD 1,926–1,990 / sq.m.) in Dubai and AED 639 / sq.ft. (USD 1,872 / sq.m.) in Abu Dhabi.
  • Where AED 250 / sq.ft. IS Valid:
  • Civil & Structural Grey-Frame (Shell/Skeleton) Only: Across mid-rise and high-rise towers, the reinforced concrete substructure + superstructure frame accounts for 38%–45% of total construction cost, which equals AED 180–270 / sq.ft. Many market participants informally quote ~AED 250/sq.ft. when referring strictly to the civil structural frame before MEP (24%–32%), Curtain Wall Façade (12%–18%), Interior Finishes (15%–25%), and Vertical Transportation (3%–6%).
  • Open-Deck Multi-Storey Car Parks (MSCP): AED 232–334 / sq.ft.
  • Industrial Pre-Engineered Steel Warehouses: AED 88–130 / sq.ft. (below the benchmark).
  • Basic Contractor-Grade Affordable Row Townhouses (G+1): Lowest specification band starts around AED 297 / sq.ft. (AED 3,200 / sq.m.).

Using a flat AED 250 / sq.ft. multiplier for a turnkey residential tower or luxury villa will underestimate total hard construction cost by +41% to +500%+. Our consolidated dataset 08_cost_benchmarks.json provides verified Low / Median / High multipliers across 15 distinct UAE building categories, plus work-package percentage splits and core material rates.

5. Recommendations for Next Phase (Platform Integration & Live Data Pipelines)

To transition from Task 01 (Static & Semi-Automated Research Repository) to the downstream UAE Construction Intelligence & BOQ Platform, we recommend a 3-track data acquisition strategy:

  • Track 1 — Automated Open-Data API Ingestion (Zero Cost, Immediate):
  • Connect directly to Dubai Pulse (api.dubaipulse.gov.ae) OAuth2 REST APIs to ingest daily updates of dld_projects-open-api (Mashrooi escrow % completion), dld_buildings-open-api, and dld_units-open-api.
  • Poll SCAD (Abu Dhabi) and DSC (Dubai) monthly construction material price bulletins to auto-adjust rebar, concrete, cement, and cable indices in the Cost Benchmark Repository.
  • Track 2 — Commercial Construction Intelligence API Licensing (B2B Data Bridge):
  • Because municipal permit portals (Build in Dubai, DMT MePS) legally restrict plot-level consultant/contractor records to plot owners, subscribe to an institutional project tracker API—specifically BNC Network (Industry Networks FZE) (tracks 23,000+ live UAE projects with named MEP/main contractors and tender stages) or MEED Projects (GlobalData)—to receive real-time webhook updates when a project transitions from Concept -> Design -> Tender -> Main Contract Award.
  • Track 3 — RICS / ICMS Parametric BOQ Engine Calibration:
  • Use the 15-category cost benchmark table in 08_cost_benchmarks.json and the 7 elemental work-package splits in 03_construction_costs_benchmarks.md rather than a single flat AED/sq.ft. rate, applying regional multipliers (1.00x Dubai, 1.01x Abu Dhabi Mainland, 1.06x–1.10x Saadiyat/Yas/Al Marjan Islands, 0.90x–0.94x Sharjah/Ajman).
